问题与改进

独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 37621545718 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

反馈证据核对

Skill 示例与实现/应用行为一致地被证实矛盾：code-and-seeds.md 第 40、69 行的 .ts seed 示例用 .js 相对说明符，而第 97 行文字却称导入 .ts（E29/E30/E40）；加载器对 .ts 源按原生 import 执行、不把 .js 说明符映射到 .ts（E2/E3），retro 记录应用照示例写 .js 后 28 个测试失败、改成 .ts 才通过（E136/E42）。这是可据源码与过程记录证实的 guidance-gap，非环境偶发。

  • 职责与公开约定：作为公开指引，code-and-seeds.md 给出可运行的 .ts seed 示例：第 40 行 import '../../server/sales-resources.js'、第 69 行 import '../../seed-data/permission-sets.js'（E29,E30），但同页第 97 行又称该 seed 导入 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts，自相矛盾（E40）。Skill 承诺示例可直接用于 App seed。 E29 E30 E40
  • 实际行为证据：task-loader 把 seed 以 pathToFileURL(...).href 交给原生 import，__rewriteRelativeImportExtension 只对 ^\.\.?/ 字面量重写且不作用于 file URL；DEFAULT_TASK_EXTENSIONS 含 .ts（E2,E3）。retro 明确 Node 原生 ESM 不做 .js→.ts 重写，.js 说明符无法解析 .ts 源（E136）。 E2 E3 E136
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用最初按示例在 seed 写 .js 相对导入，测试阶段失败；改为 '../../seed-data/permission-sets.ts' 后通过（E136），冻结代码正是 .ts 形式（E42）。说明应用是照文档示例装配才触发该问题。 E136 E42
  • 环境因素：触发条件是 Node 24 原生类型剥离直接执行 .ts seed，这属于包默认加载路径而非环境误配；retro 说明 vitest 外置 @nocobase/db 时跑的是原始 .ts，且与 tsc-alias 构建期改写不同（E136,E3）。无需外部服务或凭据即可复现。 E136 E3
  • 工厂因素：未发现工厂提示、注入配置或模板覆盖导致该矛盾；矛盾位于被评审 Skill 文本本身与其消费的加载器实现之间（E29,E30,E2,E3）。缺少任何指向工厂材料的证据，故不归因工厂。 E29 E30 E2 E3
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：加载器已支持 .ts 任务扩展（E2），应用改用 .ts 说明符即可正常运行（E42），说明修正文档/说明符即可，无需新增框架能力，支持建议类定性。 E2 E42

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

code-and-seeds.md 两处示例从 .ts 种子相对导入 .js：../../server/sales-resources.js（第 40 行）与 ../../seed-data/permission-sets.js（第 69 行）；同页第 97 行文字却说该种子导入 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts，文档自相矛盾。@nocobase/db 的种子加载器把 .ts 源以 file URL 交给原生 import，__rewriteRelativeImportExtension 只重写 ^\.\.?/ 的字面量、不匹配 file:// URL，因此 .js 说明符解析不到实际 .ts 文件。

## Share declarations, not runtime instances

Keep a portable feature module such as `server/sales-resources.ts` with `defineCompositeResource` declarations. Put permission-set values in `database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts`, importing the same resource references. The corresponding code for a customer-owned feature can live under its own `sales/` directory. Use that feature's names consistently.

```ts
import { definePermissionSet } from '@nocobase/authorization/permission-sets';
import { quotes } from '../../server/sales-resources.js';

export const engineer = definePermissionSet('sales-engineer')
  .title('Sales engineer')
  .grant({
    resource: { type: 'page', id: 'sales.quotes' },
    actions: [{ action: 'access' }],

packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:1–12E2

__rewriteRelativeImportExtension 仅对 /^\.\.?\// 的字符串重写扩展名，并声明 DEFAULT_TASK_EXTENSIONS 含 .ts。

var __rewriteRelativeImportExtension = (this && this.__rewriteRelativeImportExtension) || function (path, preserveJsx) {
    if (typeof path === "string" && /^\.\.?\//.test(path)) {
        return path.replace(/\.(tsx)$|((?:\.d)?)((?:\.[^./]+?)?)\.([cm]?)ts$/i, function (m, tsx, d, ext, cm) {
            return tsx ? preserveJsx ? ".jsx" : ".js" : d && (!ext || !cm) ? m : (d + ext + "." + cm.toLowerCase() + "js");
        });
    }
    return path;
};
import { readdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { basename, extname } from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
export const DEFAULT_TASK_EXTENSIONS = ['.js', '.mjs', '.cjs', '.ts'];
packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:54–64E3

importTaskDefinition 用 pathToFileURL(filePath).href 传给原生 import，file URL 不匹配扩展名重写正则。

    return basename(fileName, extname(fileName));
}
/** The mtime query keeps a re-read after an edit from serving a cached module. */
export async function importTaskDefinition(filePath, mtimeMs) {
    const url = pathToFileURL(filePath);
    url.searchParams.set('mtime', String(Math.trunc(mtimeMs)));
    const module = await import(__rewriteRelativeImportExtension(url.href));
    return module.default;
}

    const seen = new Map();

### [轻微][指引错误] 权限集合/种子示例以 .js 相对导入 .ts 源，照做会在种子加载时解析失败

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/db`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.17）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

Skill 示例与实现/应用行为一致地被证实矛盾：code-and-seeds.md 第 40、69 行的 .ts seed 示例用 .js 相对说明符，而第 97 行文字却称导入 .ts（E29/E30/E40）；加载器对 .ts 源按原生 import 执行、不把 .js 说明符映射到 .ts（E2/E3），retro 记录应用照示例写 .js 后 28 个测试失败、改成 .ts 才通过（E136/E42）。这是可据源码与过程记录证实的 guidance-gap，非环境偶发。

- 职责与公开约定：作为公开指引，code-and-seeds.md 给出可运行的 .ts seed 示例：第 40 行 import '../../server/sales-resources.js'、第 69 行 import '../../seed-data/permission-sets.js'（E29,E30），但同页第 97 行又称该 seed 导入 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts，自相矛盾（E40）。Skill 承诺示例可直接用于 App seed。；证据：E29（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:34–46）、E30（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:66–76）、E40（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:95–97）
- 实际行为证据：task-loader 把 seed 以 pathToFileURL(...).href 交给原生 import，__rewriteRelativeImportExtension 只对 ^\.\.?/ 字面量重写且不作用于 file URL；DEFAULT_TASK_EXTENSIONS 含 .ts（E2,E3）。retro 明确 Node 原生 ESM 不做 .js→.ts 重写，.js 说明符无法解析 .ts 源（E136）。；证据：E2（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:1–12）、E3（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:54–64）、E136（artifacts/retro.json:30–36）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用最初按示例在 seed 写 .js 相对导入，测试阶段失败；改为 '../../seed-data/permission-sets.ts' 后通过（E136），冻结代码正是 .ts 形式（E42）。说明应用是照文档示例装配才触发该问题。；证据：E136（artifacts/retro.json:30–36）、E42（app/database/main/seeds/202610150101_crm_permission_sets.ts:1–11）
- 环境因素：触发条件是 Node 24 原生类型剥离直接执行 .ts seed，这属于包默认加载路径而非环境误配；retro 说明 vitest 外置 @nocobase/db 时跑的是原始 .ts，且与 tsc-alias 构建期改写不同（E136,E3）。无需外部服务或凭据即可复现。；证据：E136（artifacts/retro.json:30–36）、E3（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:54–64）

code-and-seeds.md 两处示例从 .ts 种子相对导入 .js：../../server/sales-resources.js（第 40 行）与 ../../seed-data/permission-sets.js（第 69 行）；同页第 97 行文字却说该种子导入 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts，文档自相矛盾。@nocobase/db 的种子加载器把 .ts 源以 file URL 交给原生 import，__rewriteRelativeImportExtension 只重写 ^\.\.?/ 的字面量、不匹配 file:// URL，因此 .js 说明符解析不到实际 .ts 文件。

**文档 / API 声称**：code-and-seeds.md:40,69 的示例用 .js 相对说明符从 .ts 种子导入同仓库 .ts 模块。

**实际观察**：task-loader.js:5-6 的重写仅对 ^\.\.?/ 生效，:60 传入的是 pathToFileURL().href；.ts 源按 .js 说明符解析失败。

**影响**：按示例写种子会加载失败；应用需把说明符改为 .ts（本次 repair-2 即如此）。

**建议改动**：把示例说明符改为 .ts（与第 97 行一致），并让 importTaskDefinition 对 .ts 源回退 .js 到 .ts。

**验收标准**：在 .ts 种子中按更新后的示例导入并成功执行 pnpm nocobase db apply；或加载器对 .ts 源自动把 .js 说明符解析到 .ts

packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/numeric/decimal.js:1–12E4

decimalString 对 null 返回 null，对 string/number/bigint 转字符串，保留数据库十进制表示。

/** Preserve the database's decimal representation without rounding or reformatting. */
export function decimalString(value) {
    if (value == null)
        return null;
    if (typeof value === 'string')
        return value;
    if (typeof value === 'number' || typeof value === 'bigint')
        return String(value);
    throw new TypeError('Expected a decimal string or numeric value.');
}
/** Decimal text utilities: never round through a JavaScript number. */
export function decimalParts(value) {
packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/numeric/decimal.js:27–45E5

decimalText/normalizeDecimal 提供十进制文本工具，避免经 JS number 舍入。

    return { coefficient, scale };
}
export function decimalText(coefficient, scale) {
    const negative = coefficient < 0n;
    const digits = (negative ? -coefficient : coefficient)
        .toString()
        .padStart(scale + 1, '0');
    const text = scale
        ? `${digits.slice(0, -scale)}.${digits.slice(-scale)}`.replace(/\.?0+$/, '')
        : digits;
    return negative && coefficient !== 0n ? `-${text}` : text;
}
export function normalizeDecimal(value) {
    if (value == null)
        return null;
    const { coefficient, scale } = decimalParts(value);
    return decimalText(coefficient, scale);
}
/** A lexicographically sortable key for SQLite's exact decimal aggregates. */

import { defineSeed } from '@nocobase/db';

export default defineSeed({
  name: '202609030002_default_order_statuses',

  async run({ repository }) {
    await repository('orderStatuses').upsertOne({
      filter: { code: 'draft' },
      create: { code: 'draft', title: 'Draft' },
      update: { title: 'Draft' },
    });

app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:126–140E27

SKILL 规定种子自包含、合并后不可变，并要求区分 migration(builder/query) 与 seed(repository)。

Each context carries its own tools, and which one to use is not a free choice — see [Repository and Query](#4-repository-and-query). In short: a migration works through `builder` and `query`, and a seed writes through `repository`.

The rules are absolute, because these files are history that has already run on other machines:

- **Self-contained.** Spell out every table, field, index, constraint and metadata operation in the file. Never import a Collection definition, model, registry or shared constant that keeps evolving — that silently changes both the behavior and the checksum of something already applied.
- **Immutable once merged.** Check with `git log -- <file>` if unsure. Before the branch is merged you may correct the file in place; after it, every change is a new, later file. Never hard-code a previous checksum to make an edited migration look untouched. Correcting it in place changes nothing on its own — it is recorded as executed, so a plain apply skips it and the database keeps the schema the old file produced. `pnpm nocobase db redo` rolls the latest batch back and applies it again.
- **The file name body matches `name`**, and the name is unique across every source in the application.
- **Declare `irreversible: true`** when there is no real reverse operation, instead of writing a `down()` that quietly does nothing.
- **A seed is idempotent**, keyed on a stable business value backed by a unique constraint, so a repeated run is a no-op — `upsertOne` is the direct way to express that. A seed context has no `builder` at all, and a seed has no rollback or truncate behavior.

A backfill belongs in the migration that makes it necessary, using `query` from the same context — not in a seed.

Both run inside a transaction by default. `transaction: false` opts out, and a failure then leaves partial work behind with no history record, so take it only when the operation genuinely cannot run in one.
app/server/crm/service.ts:350–366E47

opportunityView 用 numberOrZero(row.amount) 在视图边界归一 decimal 值，注释说明 contract 声明为 number。

   * contract declares `amount` as a number, so it is normalised at this
   * boundary rather than leaking the storage representation to clients.
   */
  function opportunityView(
    row: OpportunityRow,
    owners: Map<string, string | null>,
    customerName: string | null,
  ): OpportunityView {
    return {
      ...row,
      amount: numberOrZero(row.amount),
      ownerName: owners.get(row.ownerId) ?? null,
      customerName,
    };
  }

  /** Finds one customer the caller may read, or reports it as missing. */

F3 · 写权限未包含 read 时内部读取抛 READ_FORBIDDEN，授权指引未点明写读耦合

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authorization · @nocobase/db · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/runtime-api.md · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/repository-routes.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

写权限缺少 read 会失败有明确实现与过程证据：composeDatabasePolicies 把未声明操作初始化为 false（E11），resolveReadNode 在 policy.read===false 时 invalid('READ_FORBIDDEN')（E7），rootReadContext 调用它（E8），createOne/updateOne 在返回行前解析读上下文（E9,E10）；retro 记录 followUpWrite 无 .read('*') 时 create/update 报 403，补 .read('*') 后恢复（E137）。授权指引示例只展示 read+update 同授，未点明写读耦合（E31,E32）。属 guidance-gap。

  • 职责与公开约定：授权指引的权限示例均同时声明 read 字段并把其用于 update 权限（E31,E32），但从未说明写操作内部需要读上下文、写授权必须包含 read；对『只需写』的直觉用法没有给出约束承诺。 E31 E32
  • 实际行为证据：composeDatabasePolicies 对每个 collection 以 read/create/update/delete 全 false 起底并只按已解析 check 覆盖（E11）；read===false 时 resolveReadNode 抛 READ_FORBIDDEN（E7），rootReadContext 复用该判定（E8），createOne/updateOne 在写校验后仍需读上下文返回行（E9,E10）。retro 记录线上实际 403（E137）。 E11 E7 E8 E9 E10 E137
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用的 followUpWrite 起初漏写 .read('*')，导致创建/更新跟进与建议批准失败；补上后通过，retro 指出其余写权限已含 .read('*')，故只有 follow-ups 失败（E137）。这是应用按缺失指引装配的后果。 E137
  • 环境因素：失败是仓库策略与授权折叠的确定性逻辑，在本地/线上均可复现，不依赖外部服务或凭据；证据为包源码与 retro（E7,E8,E9,E10,E137）。 E7 E8 E9 E10 E137
  • 工厂因素：未发现工厂提示、构建或模板覆盖导致；缺口在被评审 authorization 指引文本（E31,E32）未说明写读耦合。 E31 E32
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：包已支持在同一写权限上声明 read（E137 中其他权限与修复后的 followUpWrite），应用只需补一个 .read('*')；因此是文档补充而非新能力，支持 guidance-gap 定性。 E137 E11

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

composeDatabasePolicies 初始 read:false（api.js:41-45）；repository.createOne/updateOne 在校验后调用 validateSelect(..., readContext(collection))（repository.js:284/372），rootReadContext 经 resolveReadNode 在 read===false 时 invalid('READ_FORBIDDEN')。指引示例都是 read+update 同时授予（runtime-api.md quoteData），未说明只写不读会在返回行时失败。

实际影响
按只需写的直觉声明权限会在运行时报 READ_FORBIDDEN，多一轮排查成本。
建议改法
在 authorization 指引显式说明写操作需要 read 授予，或在 repository API 文档标注返回行依赖读上下文。

packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/policy/enforce.js:1–16E7

resolveReadNode 在 policy.read === false 时抛 READ_FORBIDDEN，禁止读即拒绝调用。

import { invalid, isPlainRecord } from '../internal/guards.js';
/**
 * Resolve the read node, rejecting the call outright when reading is forbidden.
 * Returns `undefined` when no Policy is bound and `true` when it adds no limits.
 */
export function resolveReadNode(policy, collection) {
    const node = policy?.read;
    if (node === false) {
        invalid('READ_FORBIDDEN', 'Reading from this Repository is forbidden by Policy.', { collection: collection.name });
    }
    return node;
}
/** The scalar field allowlist of the bound read node, or `undefined` when unbound. */
export function policyReadFields(policy) {
    const node = policy?.read;
    return node && typeof node === 'object' ? node.fields : undefined;
packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/policy/enforce.js:131–149E8

rootReadContext 调用 resolveReadNode 构造读上下文，restrictedReadContext 按授权关系扩展可命名字段。

}
export const UNRESTRICTED_READ = Object.freeze({
    kind: 'unrestricted',
});
/** Build the root read context, rejecting the call when reading is forbidden. */
export function rootReadContext(policy, collection) {
    const node = resolveReadNode(policy, collection);
    return node === undefined || node === true
        ? UNRESTRICTED_READ
        : restrictedReadContext(collection, node);
}
/**
 * Build a restricted context, widening the nameable fields by the foreign keys
 * the authorized relations already expose.
 */

    return {
        kind: 'restricted',
        fields: node.fields,

    async createOne(options) {
        const policyInput = options.writePolicy === undefined ? true : options.writePolicy;
        assertWriteEnabled(policyInput);
        const writePolicy = policyInput === true ? true : normalizeWritePolicy(policyInput, 'create');
        const collection = await this.collection();
        assertWritableCollection(collection);
        // Before the payload is looked at: a Policy that forbids this operation
        // refuses the call outright, so an empty or malformed body is reported as
        // forbidden rather than as invalid input. The caller learns nothing about
        // the shape of a write they may not perform.
        resolveWriteShapeNode(this.options.policy, 'create', collection);
        await validateWritePolicyMetadata(this.options.collections, collection, writePolicy);
        const createValues = applyCreateDefaults(this.options.policy, evaluateValues(options.values));
        const mutation = await normalizeModelMutation(this.options.collections, collection, createValues.values, 'createOne', 1, { nodes: 0, clientKeys: new Set(), context: options.context });
        this.assertPolicyMutationFields(collection, mutation, 'create', ['values'], createValues.protectedFields);
        assertMutationWritePolicy(mutation, writePolicy, ['values'], [], 'create', collection.name);

            collection,

packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/api.js:35–66E11

composeDatabasePolicies 只翻译已解析检查，每个 collection 策略初始 read/create/update/delete 均为 false。

}
/** Translate resolved checks only; this never runs authorization again. */
export function composeDatabasePolicies(checks) {
    const policies = Object.create(null);
    for (const check of checks) {
        if (check.resource.type !== 'database.collection')
            continue;
        const policy = (policies[check.resource.id] ??= {
            read: false,
            create: false,
            update: false,
            delete: false,
        });
        switch (check.action) {
            case 'read':
                policy.read = foldDecision('read', check.decision);
                break;
            case 'create':
                policy.create = foldDecision('create', check.decision);
                break;
            case 'update':
                policy.update = foldDecision('update', check.decision);
                break;
            case 'delete':
                policy.delete = foldDecision('delete', check.decision);
                break;
        }
    }
    return policies;
}
function foldDecision(action, decision) {
    if (decision.effect === 'deny')

repository-routes 用 defineDatabasePermission 定义 read 字段，再由复合资源 action grant。

```ts
import { defineCompositeResource } from '@nocobase/authorization/core';
import { defineDatabasePermission } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server';

const projectData = defineDatabasePermission((p) =>
  p
    .collection('projects')
    .read(['id', 'title', 'region', 'ownerId', 'confidential', 'notes']),
);

export const projectResource = defineCompositeResource(
  'sales.projects',
  (resource) =>
    resource
      .title('Projects')
      .action('view', (action) => action.grant('projects', projectData))
      .action('edit', (action) =>
        action.grant('projects', projectData.update(['title', 'notes'])),
      ),
);
```

The project resource declares `view` with project read fields and `edit` with read plus update of `title` and `notes`, both under one `projects` scope. The following route module imports that application-owned declaration. The route factory owns authentication and the body limit; register its exported contribution in the App's server routes.

### [轻微][指引缺失] 写权限未包含 read 时内部读取抛 READ_FORBIDDEN，授权指引未点明写读耦合

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.24）、`@nocobase/db`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.17）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/runtime-api.md`、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/repository-routes.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

写权限缺少 read 会失败有明确实现与过程证据：composeDatabasePolicies 把未声明操作初始化为 false（E11），resolveReadNode 在 policy.read===false 时 invalid('READ_FORBIDDEN')（E7），rootReadContext 调用它（E8），createOne/updateOne 在返回行前解析读上下文（E9,E10）；retro 记录 followUpWrite 无 .read('*') 时 create/update 报 403，补 .read('*') 后恢复（E137）。授权指引示例只展示 read+update 同授，未点明写读耦合（E31,E32）。属 guidance-gap。

- 职责与公开约定：授权指引的权限示例均同时声明 read 字段并把其用于 update 权限（E31,E32），但从未说明写操作内部需要读上下文、写授权必须包含 read；对『只需写』的直觉用法没有给出约束承诺。；证据：E31（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/runtime-api.md:68–102）、E32（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/repository-routes.md:26–52）
- 实际行为证据：composeDatabasePolicies 对每个 collection 以 read/create/update/delete 全 false 起底并只按已解析 check 覆盖（E11）；read===false 时 resolveReadNode 抛 READ_FORBIDDEN（E7），rootReadContext 复用该判定（E8），createOne/updateOne 在写校验后仍需读上下文返回行（E9,E10）。retro 记录线上实际 403（E137）。；证据：E11（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/api.js:35–66）、E7（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/policy/enforce.js:1–16）、E8（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/policy/enforce.js:131–149）、E9（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/repository.js:266–292）、E10（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/repository.js:353–380）、E137（artifacts/retro.json:62–68）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用的 followUpWrite 起初漏写 .read('*')，导致创建/更新跟进与建议批准失败；补上后通过，retro 指出其余写权限已含 .read('*')，故只有 follow-ups 失败（E137）。这是应用按缺失指引装配的后果。；证据：E137（artifacts/retro.json:62–68）
- 环境因素：失败是仓库策略与授权折叠的确定性逻辑，在本地/线上均可复现，不依赖外部服务或凭据；证据为包源码与 retro（E7,E8,E9,E10,E137）。；证据：E7（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/policy/enforce.js:1–16）、E8（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/policy/enforce.js:131–149）、E9（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/repository.js:266–292）、E10（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/repository.js:353–380）、E137（artifacts/retro.json:62–68）
- 工厂因素：未发现工厂提示、构建或模板覆盖导致；缺口在被评审 authorization 指引文本（E31,E32）未说明写读耦合。；证据：E31（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/runtime-api.md:68–102）、E32（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/repository-routes.md:26–52）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：包已支持在同一写权限上声明 read（E137 中其他权限与修复后的 followUpWrite），应用只需补一个 .read('*')；因此是文档补充而非新能力，支持 guidance-gap 定性。；证据：E137（artifacts/retro.json:62–68）、E11（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/api.js:35–66）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

composeDatabasePolicies 初始 read:false（api.js:41-45）；repository.createOne/updateOne 在校验后调用 validateSelect(..., readContext(collection))（repository.js:284/372），rootReadContext 经 resolveReadNode 在 read===false 时 invalid('READ_FORBIDDEN')。指引示例都是 read+update 同时授予（runtime-api.md quoteData），未说明只写不读会在返回行时失败。

**影响**：按只需写的直觉声明权限会在运行时报 READ_FORBIDDEN，多一轮排查成本。

**建议改动**：在 authorization 指引显式说明写操作需要 read 授予，或在 repository API 文档标注返回行依赖读上下文。

**验收标准**：指引含最小可运行示例或明确说明；或写操作不再要求 read 即可返回结果

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/policy/enforce.js:1–16` — resolveReadNode 在 policy.read === false 时抛 READ_FORBIDDEN，禁止读即拒绝调用。
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/policy/enforce.js:131–149` — rootReadContext 调用 resolveReadNode 构造读上下文，restrictedReadContext 按授权关系扩展可命名字段。
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/repository.js:266–292` — createOne 先断言写策略，再在返回行前解析读上下文。
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/repository.js:353–380` — updateOne 与 createOne 同构：写策略校验后仍需读上下文以返回更新后的行。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/api.js:35–66` — composeDatabasePolicies 只翻译已解析检查，每个 collection 策略初始 read/create/update/delete 均为 false。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/runtime-api.md:68–102` — runtime-api 示例的 quoteData 同时声明 read 字段并作为 update 权限的 grant。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/repository-routes.md:26–52` — repository-routes 用 defineDatabasePermission 定义 read 字段，再由复合资源 action grant。

    ### [轻微][易用性改进] @nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server 只重导出部分 core 授权类型，消费方需自行判断类型来源

    应用绕行与代价

    改进后的验收标准

    以 CRM 构建场景（Issue #646）评估 NocoBase3 基础框架：数据建模/迁移/种子、行级与复合资源授权、通知与定时任务、应用装配与路由、AI 助手接入五个能力单元。框架的声明式授权与 token/ServiceProvider 装配是明显强项；主要摩擦来自文档与实现的边界未对齐（种子 .js 相对导入、decimal 读取类型、写权限隐含读权限），以及应用侧未把已安装的通知/调度与 AI 插件接入运行时。

    E28

    需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

    管理客户、联系人、商机和跟进记录，商机阶段流转，客户详情关联展示。直接支持
    框架职责与业务边界
    Collection/字段/关联与迁移由 @nocobase/db 的 builder 与 defineMigration 负责；业务字段、状态机与页面由应用。
    推荐使用方式
    nocobase-db SKILL §2–3：migration 用 builder+query，seed 用 repository；migrations.md 指明 database/main/migrations 与 db apply。
    Agent 实际使用
    应用在 database/main/migrations 用 defineMigration+builder.createCollection 建表并给每表加 ownerId；verify-4 数据流通过。
    缺口归因
    未记录能力缺口
    E41 E26 E28
    初始数据（默认权限集合）可重复部署且不覆盖管理员修改。正常组合
    框架职责与业务边界
    defineSeed/幂等与 db apply 由 @nocobase/db 负责；具体种子数据由应用。
    推荐使用方式
    defineSeed 自包含、可重入，种子写 repository/query。
    Agent 实际使用
    应用写了 permission-sets seed 并先查询存在性；但示例中的 .js 相对导入不可用，实际改成 .ts。
    缺口归因
    未记录能力缺口
    E42 E43 E1 E26
    商机金额可精确存储与展示（首页预计成交金额）。正常组合
    框架职责与业务边界
    db 负责 decimal 的存储与读取解码契约；展示与边界转换由应用。
    推荐使用方式
    SKILL 只列出 decimal builder 与 precision/scale，未说明读取解码结果类型。
    Agent 实际使用
    服务层 opportunityView 用 numberOrZero(row.amount) 在边界归一；verify-3 列表 Amount 显示 "—"，repair-3 修复后 verify-4 通过。

    NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/authorization · @nocobase/app-plugin-authorization

    ⌄

    authorization 提供 defineCompositeResource/definePermissionSet，app-plugin-authorization 提供 defineDatabasePermission/recordAccess 与 page/settings/ui 授权；应用只需声明 ownerId 数据域选项与页面 ID，即可让销售/主管获得不同行级范围。

    提供的帮助
    避免应用自写 SQL 过滤与页面守卫，权限模型集中可审计。
    应保留的能力
    保持该声明式模型，并在 Skill 中补充写读耦合与类型导入位置等边界说明。
    E13 E14 E11 E44 E45 E46

    问题、误导与建议统一收录在问题与改进，不在此重复展示。

    本次评审的覆盖限制
    • 本次为冻结快照的只读评审：未安装依赖、未运行应用、未修改任何被评审文件；所有结论基于 review-files.json 中的文件片段与原始过程日志。
    • UI 未评审：本会话模型无法渲染 PNG，虽然 verify-4 生成了 dashboard/customers/opportunities 等截图，但未做视觉一致性判断，ui.status 保持 not-reviewed、score 为 null。
    • 输入快照有 3 个文件因预算/非普通文件/不可读被省略，未据此推断其实现；过程证据按 invocation 覆盖率与显式错误信号核对，错误信号本身不等于框架缺陷。
    • 未逐字节通读各库实现：可靠性分数基于所选实现片段与公开入口，未覆盖并发、多数据库连接与运行时引擎等路径。
    • M3 通知/调度与 M5 AI 助手的可靠性维度为 null：未阅读投递/执行/agent 运行时实现，且本次运行日志中没有 AI 调用轨迹。
    • 应用是否尝试过接入通知/调度与 AI 插件无法从日志确认，F7 的 confidence 记为 suspected。
    • 快照未包含 3 个超出预算、非普通文件或不可读取的文件；未据此确认其实现。

async function loadSeedFile(packageName, filePath, fileName, manifest) {
    const [source, fileStat] = await Promise.all([
        readFile(filePath, 'utf8'),
        stat(filePath),
    ]);
    const checksums = resolveTaskChecksum(filePath, source, manifest);
    const seed = await importTaskDefinition(filePath, fileStat.mtimeMs);
    validateSeedDefinition(seed, filePath, fileName);
    return {
        packageName,
        name: seed.name,
        filePath,
        fileName,
        ...checksums,
        seed,
    };
}

    E2 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:1–12

    __rewriteRelativeImportExtension 仅对 /^\.\.?\// 的字符串重写扩展名，并声明 DEFAULT_TASK_EXTENSIONS 含 .ts。

    var __rewriteRelativeImportExtension = (this && this.__rewriteRelativeImportExtension) || function (path, preserveJsx) {
    if (typeof path === "string" && /^\.\.?\//.test(path)) {
        return path.replace(/\.(tsx)$|((?:\.d)?)((?:\.[^./]+?)?)\.([cm]?)ts$/i, function (m, tsx, d, ext, cm) {
            return tsx ? preserveJsx ? ".jsx" : ".js" : d && (!ext || !cm) ? m : (d + ext + "." + cm.toLowerCase() + "js");
        });
    }
    return path;
};

    E3 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:54–64

/** The mtime query keeps a re-read after an edit from serving a cached module. */
export async function importTaskDefinition(filePath, mtimeMs) {
    const url = pathToFileURL(filePath);
    url.searchParams.set('mtime', String(Math.trunc(mtimeMs)));
    const module = await import(__rewriteRelativeImportExtension(url.href));
    return module.default;
}

    E4 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/numeric/decimal.js:1–12

    decimalString 对 null 返回 null，对 string/number/bigint 转字符串，保留数据库十进制表示。

    /** Preserve the database's decimal representation without rounding or reformatting. */
export function decimalString(value) {
    if (value == null)
        return null;
    if (typeof value === 'string')
        return value;
    if (typeof value === 'number' || typeof value === 'bigint')
        return String(value);
    throw new TypeError('Expected a decimal string or numeric value.');
}
/** Decimal text utilities: never round through a JavaScript number. */
export function decimalParts(value) {

    E5 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/numeric/decimal.js:27–45

    decimalText/normalizeDecimal 提供十进制文本工具，避免经 JS number 舍入。

        return { coefficient, scale };
}
export function decimalText(coefficient, scale) {
    const negative = coefficient < 0n;
    const digits = (negative ? -coefficient : coefficient)
        .toString()
        .padStart(scale + 1, '0');
    const text = scale
        ? `${digits.slice(0, -scale)}.${digits.slice(-scale)}`.replace(/\.?0+$/, '')
        : digits;
    return negative && coefficient !== 0n ? `-${text}` : text;
}
export function normalizeDecimal(value) {
    if (value == null)
        return null;
    const { coefficient, scale } = decimalParts(value);
    return decimalText(coefficient, scale);
}
/** A lexicographically sortable key for SQLite's exact decimal aggregates. */

/**
 * Resolve the read node, rejecting the call outright when reading is forbidden.
 * Returns `undefined` when no Policy is bound and `true` when it adds no limits.
 */
export function resolveReadNode(policy, collection) {
    const node = policy?.read;
    if (node === false) {
        invalid('READ_FORBIDDEN', 'Reading from this Repository is forbidden by Policy.', { collection: collection.name });
    }
    return node;
}
/** The scalar field allowlist of the bound read node, or `undefined` when unbound. */
export function policyReadFields(policy) {
    const node = policy?.read;
    return node && typeof node === 'object' ? node.fields : undefined;

export const UNRESTRICTED_READ = Object.freeze({
    kind: 'unrestricted',
});
/** Build the root read context, rejecting the call when reading is forbidden. */
export function rootReadContext(policy, collection) {

    return node === undefined || node === true
        ? UNRESTRICTED_READ

    E11 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/api.js:35–66

    composeDatabasePolicies 只翻译已解析检查，每个 collection 策略初始 read/create/update/delete 均为 false。

    }
/** Translate resolved checks only; this never runs authorization again. */
export function composeDatabasePolicies(checks) {
    const policies = Object.create(null);
    for (const check of checks) {
        if (check.resource.type !== 'database.collection')
            continue;
        const policy = (policies[check.resource.id] ??= {
            read: false,
            create: false,
            update: false,
            delete: false,
        });
        switch (check.action) {
            case 'read':
                policy.read = foldDecision('read', check.decision);
                break;
            case 'create':
                policy.create = foldDecision('create', check.decision);
                break;
            case 'update':
                policy.update = foldDecision('update', check.decision);
                break;
            case 'delete':
                policy.delete = foldDecision('delete', check.decision);
                break;
        }
    }
    return policies;
}
function foldDecision(action, decision) {
    if (decision.effect === 'deny')

    E13 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/composite.js:112–140

    defineCompositeResource 校验名称并构建 action 列表，validateComposite 拒绝无 action 的定义。

}
/** Builds a composite; `authz.compositeResources.define` registers it. */
export function defineCompositeResource(name, configure) {
    if (!name)
        throw new TypeError('A composite resource needs a name');
    const result = configure(new CompositeResourceBuilder({ name, title: name, actions: [] }));
    return new CompositeResourceBuilder(result.build());
}
function validateComposite(definition) {
    if (!definition.name)
        throw new TypeError('A composite resource needs a name');
    const names = definition.actions.map((action) => action.name);
    if (!names.length ||
        names.some((name) => !name) ||
        new Set(names).size !== names.length)
        throw new TypeError(`Composite resource ${definition.name} needs unique, nonempty actions`);
    for (const action of definition.actions)
        validateCompositeAction(action);
    return definition;
}
function validateCompositeAction(action) {
    if (!action.grants.length)
        throw new TypeError(`Composite resource action ${action.name} grants nothing`);
    for (const grant of action.grants)
        if (grant.resource.type === COMPOSITE_RESOURCE_TYPE)
            throw new TypeError(`Composite resource action ${action.name} cannot compose another composite resource: ${grant.resource.id}`);
    const scopes = action.dataScopes ?? [];

    E14 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/plugins/permission-sets/builder.js:1–25

    definePermissionSet/PermissionSetBuilder 以 title/grant 链式构建权限集合数据，不写存储。

    /** Builds Permission Set data without writing to a store. */
export class PermissionSetBuilder {
    definition;
    constructor(definition) {
        this.definition = structuredClone(definition);
    }
    title(title) {
        return new PermissionSetBuilder({ ...this.definition, title });
    }
    grant(...grants) {
        return new PermissionSetBuilder({
            ...this.definition,
            grants: [...this.definition.grants, ...grants],
        });
    }
    build() {
        return structuredClone(this.definition);
    }
}
export function definePermissionSet(key) {
    if (!key)
        throw new TypeError('A Permission Set needs a key');
    return new PermissionSetBuilder({ key, grants: [] });
}

    E27 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:126–140

    SKILL 规定种子自包含、合并后不可变，并要求区分 migration(builder/query) 与 seed(repository)。

    Each context carries its own tools, and which one to use is not a free choice — see [Repository and Query](#4-repository-and-query). In short: a migration works through `builder` and `query`, and a seed writes through `repository`.

The rules are absolute, because these files are history that has already run on other machines:

- **Self-contained.** Spell out every table, field, index, constraint and metadata operation in the file. Never import a Collection definition, model, registry or shared constant that keeps evolving — that silently changes both the behavior and the checksum of something already applied.
- **Immutable once merged.** Check with `git log -- <file>` if unsure. Before the branch is merged you may correct the file in place; after it, every change is a new, later file. Never hard-code a previous checksum to make an edited migration look untouched. Correcting it in place changes nothing on its own — it is recorded as executed, so a plain apply skips it and the database keeps the schema the old file produced. `pnpm nocobase db redo` rolls the latest batch back and applies it again.
- **The file name body matches `name`**, and the name is unique across every source in the application.
- **Declare `irreversible: true`** when there is no real reverse operation, instead of writing a `down()` that quietly does nothing.
- **A seed is idempotent**, keyed on a stable business value backed by a unique constraint, so a repeated run is a no-op — `upsertOne` is the direct way to express that. A seed context has no `builder` at all, and a seed has no rollback or truncate behavior.

A backfill belongs in the migration that makes it necessary, using `query` from the same context — not in a seed.

Both run inside a transaction by default. `transaction: false` opts out, and a failure then leaves partial work behind with no history record, so take it only when the operation genuinely cannot run in one.

    原文件 SHA-256：d33a58740d6a2921689e56f964c081354574ed857389118e319719bde6936991

    E28 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/migrations.md:1–20

    migrations.md 指明应用侧 database/main/{migrations,seeds} 与 pnpm nocobase db apply，并转交 nocobase-db Skill。

    # Migrations and seeds

This page is the application side: where the directories live, how `pnpm nocobase db apply` runs them, how additional connections configure their tasks, and what to do about checksum drift and compiled manifests.

How to write the files themselves — the migration and seed shape, the immutability and self-containment rules, the Collection Builder, and which of `builder`, `query` and `repository` a task context should reach for — belongs to the package that owns them. Read `.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md` sections 2 and 3 before writing one, and run `pnpm nocobase skills sync` if that file is missing.

Migrations under `database/main/migrations/` are the application's schema history. Seeds under `database/main/seeds/` insert records the application requires to run. Migrations create structure. Seeds never do.

`database/tsconfig.json` extends the server configuration so editor tooling and ESLint recognize migrations and seeds as Node source. Keep it in place when adding connection directories.

## Running

```bash
pnpm nocobase db apply
```

This applies pending migrations and then pending seeds for `database.default`, including registered plugins, ordered by name across all sources. Both halves run only what is pending, so on an already-migrated database it applies seeds alone. The template defaults to `main`. Plugin migrations, seeds and runtime default reads/writes always use this same connection; changing `database.default` changes the application system database.

Seeds are the second half of that run, so the structure a seed writes into must already exist from an earlier migration. Keep seed data fixed and reproducible — no current timestamps or random values in identifying fields — and never let a repeat run silently overwrite data a user has edited.

    E33 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md:68–106

    services-and-jobs 说明 client/service-provider 模式与后台任务放置选择。

    The client has the same pattern. `client/service-provider.ts` holds application startup logic. In React components and custom Hooks, use `useApiClient()` to resolve the application's HTTP client and `useService(token)` to resolve other services:

```tsx
const api = useApiClient();
const realtime = useService(realtimeClientToken);
```

## Choose where background work runs

| The work                                                                                                                                               | Use                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Runs once, outside the request that caused it, and retries on failure                                                                                  | `JobExecutor`, below — the default                                                   |
| Recurs by cron or interval, and nobody tracks it in the UI                                                                                             | `ScheduleExecutor`, in [Work that runs on a schedule](#work-that-runs-on-a-schedule) |
| Recurs, and administrators view, enable, disable and track it                                                                                          | A Scheduler schedule, as the Scheduler plugin's Skill describes                      |
| Needs a delay, a priority, a job ID that deduplicates, an atomic batch, a global rate limit, cancelling a running job, or several handlers per message | A queue, in [Queues](#queues)                                                        |

Both executors come from the application's jobs service and run on the `jobs` configuration; queues run on the separate `queue` configuration. An application running more than one instance sets `jobs.default`, and `queue.default` if it uses queues, to a `redis` configuration.

## Ordinary one-off tasks

Use `JobExecutor` from `@nocobase/jobs` for immediate one-off tasks with payload-only classes. Resolve the existing `jobExecutorServiceToken` from `@nocobase/app-server/jobs` and call `getJobExecutor(scope, name?)`; do not add another provider, token, global registry or service container.

```ts
import { jobExecutorServiceToken } from '@nocobase/app-server/jobs';
import { Job, type JobExecutionContext } from '@nocobase/jobs';

class PublishDocument extends Job<{ url: string; documentId: string }> {
  public static readonly jobName: string = 'documents.publish';

  public async execute({ jobId, signal }: JobExecutionContext): Promise<void> {
    const response = await fetch(this.payload.url, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Idempotency-Key': jobId },
      body: JSON.stringify({ documentId: this.payload.documentId }),
      signal,
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Publication failed: ${response.status}`);
  }
}

    原文件 SHA-256：462d47c73ef76e47aabfd8f94e96b0900ba9414d05a41e7115a92f430be2705e

    E34 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:10–32

    scheduler SKILL 区分 UI 可观测的 Scheduler 与代码定义的调度，并说明应用拥有业务逻辑与 schedule 声明。

    Use the installed version's public exports, without importing plugin internals. The application owns business logic, schedule declarations, Providers, permissions, and deployment timing. Scheduler owns scheduling projections, trigger history, and execution state. The plugin's `skills/` directory is the source of truth; do not edit the synchronized application copy under `.agents/skills/`.

## Decide Whether to Use Scheduler

The primary reason to define tasks through Scheduler is **UI observability for business administrators**. Developers define schedules and execution logic in code. Business administrators use the application's task list and detail pages to view schedules, track individual executions, and enable or disable tasks when authorized.

Tasks that do not need to be viewed and tracked through the UI must not be managed by the Scheduler plugin. Give recurring ones a `ScheduleExecutor` of their own from the application's jobs service (`jobExecutorServiceToken` in `@nocobase/app-server/jobs`), and one-off background work a `JobExecutor`; the application development Skill describes both. For administrator-visible tasks, deliver Client registration, access permissions, meaningful task names, and accurate final execution status together; successful server-side dispatch alone is incomplete.

## Then Choose the Execution Model

After deciding to use Scheduler, choose according to business complexity:

| Scenario                                                                                  | Choice          | Implementation boundary                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Periodic cleanup, cache refresh, a single report, or one business Service call            | Own target type | Short operations may complete directly; hand lengthy work to a `JobExecutor`                               |
| Staged processing, branches, persisted node state, or node-level diagnostics              | `workflow`      | Scheduler determines when to trigger; Workflow orchestrates the process and nodes call typed business code |
| A simple operation that happens hourly                                                    | Own target type | Cron alone is not a reason to introduce Workflow                                                           |
| Immediate asynchronous execution                                                          | `JobExecutor`   | No Cron Schedule is needed                                                                                 |
| A one-time delay, such as ten minutes after a request                                     | Queue `delay`   | No Cron Schedule is needed; `JobExecutor` has no delay                                                     |
| Another execution system with its own references, status queries, and completion protocol | Own target type | The same `registerTarget()` call, with `inspect()` and completion reporting for work that finishes later   |

Every schedule points at a registered target; `registerTarget()` is the target extension surface, and there is no built-in target type. `workflow` means `target.type: 'workflow'`, registered by the Workflow plugin, not an additional job wrapping a workflow. An application's own targets do not depend on the Workflow plugin. Neither approach guarantees exactly-once external business effects; design business idempotency for both.

    原文件 SHA-256：4399fba36c98661ac61c08026539960c74de06809e28d2686f844a8dce704eb9

    E35 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-notification/SKILL.md:13–28

    notification SKILL 要求先检查已注册插件与通知配置，并指向概念/集成/发送参考。

    ---

# Notification development

Inspect the application's registered plugins and effective notification configuration before changing integration code. Missing required input blocks an actual send; use the recipient and Channel scope already authorized in the conversation and ask only for missing information.

1. Read [Notification concepts](references/notification-concepts.md) for package ownership and delivery status semantics.
2. For plugin registration, configuration, migrations, or test APIs, read [Integration and configuration](references/integration-and-configuration.md). Configure a Channel name mapped to one flat Provider configuration.
3. For sending or typed consumers, read [Sending notifications](references/sending-notifications.md). Resolve `notificationServiceToken`; construct complete messages keyed by Channel name and use native recipient addresses. Validate the intended external effect before sending.
4. For failures and retries, read [Delivery diagnostics](references/delivery-diagnostics.md). Preserve Notification, Delivery, Attempt, and retry-audit history; distinguish accepted submission from final delivery.
5. For custom transports, read [Channel and Provider extensions](references/channel-and-provider-extensions.md). Register globally unique Provider definitions through `notificationExtensionRegistryToken` and retain the queue/lease/retry lifecycle.
6. Verify changed packages and consumers with lint, typecheck, tests, and build. Cover atomic validation, independent delivery, recipient rules, and retries bound to original identities.

The test API requires authentication and its test header; submission additionally requires `notification:test` `send`. A test notification is a real send. Select only the Channel and provide the fields required by its message type. Credentials stay on the server.

## High-impact actions and rollback

    E36 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-ai-employee/SKILL.md:6–32

    ai-employee SKILL 界定 App/插件各自拥有物，并提示忽略 NocoBase 2 的同名 AI Skill。

    ---

# AI Employee in a NocoBase App

This Skill covers the application-owned half of `@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee`: what the App writes, where it writes it, and what the plugin already does so the App does not rebuild it. Work inside a CLI-created App (`pnpm create @nocobase/app <name>`); the current directory is the App root when it holds `client/`, `server/`, and `package.json`.

Ignore any globally installed NocoBase 2 AI Skill. They answer to the same words — "AI employee", "AI manager" — and describe a different product with different APIs.

## Ownership

```text
App owns       employees, backend tools, skills, config.yml, page composition,
               business collections, business authorization, invocation timing,
               any LLM provider it adds
Plugin owns    chat transport and SSE, conversation persistence, tool approval,
               attachment parsing, built-in tools, skills and LLM providers,
               /api/aiEmployees and /api/aiEmployee
Public entry   @nocobase/ai-employee root, @nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee/server,
               pnpm nocobase ai-employee models/test,
               the nocobase-ai Registry item installed at client/extensions/nocobase-ai
Do not bypass  plugin server/agent source paths, @nocobase/ai-employee/src/*,
               the synchronized copy under .agents/skills/
```

An importable subpath is not a runtime contribution. `@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee/server` exports tokens and the registrar base class; importing it never registers anything. Registration happens in the App's own Provider, once, in `boot()`.

Import a token from the package that created it. `createServiceToken` is keyed by object identity, so a second `createServiceToken('ai-manager')` is a different key that resolves nothing.

    E37 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-ai-employee/SKILL.md:66–82

    SKILL 给出端到端最短路径：配置 LLM、先写 tool 再写 skill、在 server/ai/index.ts 用 AIResourceRegistrar 聚合注册。

    For LLM setup tasks, read [Configure LLM services](references/llm-configuration.md) before changing configuration.

## Shortest end-to-end path

Do these in order; each step depends on the one before it.

1. **Configure the LLM service and models** using [Configure LLM services](references/llm-configuration.md): follow its safe configuration update, startup, and single-model test rules.
2. **Write the tool first, then the skill that names it.** A tool is registered in code; a Skill references it by name and cannot define one. `ai/skills/` holds Skills only.
3. **Aggregate and register.** Static-import employees and tools in `server/ai/index.ts` through a subclass of `AIResourceRegistrar`, then call `registerAIResources()` from an App `ServiceProvider.boot()` with `aiManagerToken`. See [server-runs.md § Register App resources](references/server-runs.md#register-app-resources).
4. **Define the employee.** `defineAIEmployee()` with a stable `username`, a `systemPrompt`, an `avatar` copied from the plugin's list, and the `skills` that bring its tools — list a tool in `tools` only when no Skill names it. See [capabilities.md § Employees](references/capabilities.md#employees).
5. **Mount a chat surface** behind the readiness gate, passing `defaultEmployee` so the page opens on the App's employee rather than whichever one sorts first — the built-in `atlas` has `sort: 0` and wins by default. Set `enableAttachments` on every chat surface unless the user has said they do not want file uploads: it defaults to `false`, and a chat that cannot take a screenshot or a document is the exception. Set `enableWebSearch` as well unless the user does not want web search: it also defaults to `false`, and its toggle is usable only on a model that searches; see [chat-surfaces.md § Web search toggle](references/chat-surfaces.md#web-search-toggle). Start from the plugin's working page for the surface, task, page context or tool renderer you are building — see [source-map.md § Working examples](references/source-map.md#working-examples) for where they are and what not to copy.
6. **Verify by observation**, not by reading the source back. Run the checks below.

## Safety

- Keep secrets out of the repository and conversation; never put them in the browser-visible `client:` block. Follow [LLM configuration safety](references/llm-configuration.md#safety) for key handling and silent model-list updates.

    E38 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:1–17

    server-routes 区分 defineApiRoutes(→/api/*) 与 defineRootRoutes(webhook)，并要求不重复 /api。

    # Server routes

HTTP endpoints live in `server/routes/` and are listed in the array `server/routes/index.ts` exports, which `server/runtime.ts` passes to the application.

## Two kinds

| Function             | Path in code     | Final URL        | For                                              |
| -------------------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `defineApiRoutes()`  | `/orders`        | `/api/orders`    | Application APIs the browser calls               |
| `defineRootRoutes()` | `/callbacks/pay` | `/callbacks/pay` | Webhooks, OAuth callbacks, protocol entry points |

Do not repeat `/api` in the path, and never write the deployment base path such as `/main` — both are added by the runtime.

Name paths, shape responses and errors, validate input and declare each route for the API document as [HTTP API design](http-api.md) describes. Every `/api` route carries a `describeRoute()` and validates its input with `apiValidator()`, both from `@nocobase/app-server/router`; a route without a declaration is a defect.

## An authenticated endpoint

    E39 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/ui-workflow.md:1–22

    ui-workflow 定义 quick/theme/full 三种前端工作流及完成标准。

    # Frontend development workflow

This is the entry point for the application's frontend work: read it before writing or changing anything under `client/` — pages, components, styles or copy. It decides which of three workflows a change takes and what counts as done:

- **Quick change**: edit directly, then run the static checks and the related tests and look at the changed element once in the browser.
- **Theme change**: change tokens or presets as [`references/theme.md`](references/theme.md) describes and verify with its theme tests and browser check.
- **Full workflow**: a design file reviewed once, development, then one independent acceptance review in the browser.

What the interface should look like is in [`ui-guidelines.md`](ui-guidelines.md); how to write the code is in [`frontend-dev.md`](frontend-dev.md). Paths in this document are relative to its own directory: [`ui-guidelines.md`](ui-guidelines.md) and [`frontend-dev.md`](frontend-dev.md) sit beside it, the topic references are under `references/`, the record templates and reviewer prompts are under `templates/`, and the screenshot tool is under `scripts/`.

## Choosing a workflow

Decide before you start, and say in one sentence in your reply which workflow you are following and why.

| Workflow      | When it applies                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Full workflow | Any one of: a new page that loads data or has forms or overlays; a new complete interaction (a create/edit flow, a detail drawer, a multi-step operation); a change to the page structure or main interactions; one change that touches the layout or interactions of several pages; the user asks to see the design first |
| Theme change  | The change is to theme tokens or presets: the primary color, fonts, density, radius or shadows of the whole application, or adding, changing or removing a preset. Follow ["Theme change"](#theme-change) below. No design file unless the user asks                                                                       |
| Quick change  | None of the above applies. For example: changing copy; adjusting styling details on one page; adding a button, a column, a form field or a filter to an existing page; fixing a UI bug; a refactor that changes neither appearance nor behavior                                                                            |

When unsure, explain the difference in one sentence (the full workflow adds a design, a design review and an independent acceptance review) and let the user choose. A quick change needs only the next section; a theme change needs only [`references/theme.md`](references/theme.md).

    E46 · app/server/providers/crm.ts:1–48

    CrmProvider 继承 ServiceProvider，register 注册 crmServiceToken，并用 authorizationToken/databaseManagerToken 装配。

    import { authorizationToken } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server';
import type { Application } from '@nocobase/app-server/application';
import { databaseManagerToken } from '@nocobase/db';
import { ServiceProvider } from '@nocobase/service-provider';

import { crm } from '../crm/resources.js';
import { createCrmService } from '../crm/service.js';
import { crmServiceToken } from '../crm/tokens.js';

/** The CRM tables as the authorization workspace lists them. */
const CRM_COLLECTION_TITLES: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = {
  crmCustomers: 'CRM customers',
  crmContacts: 'CRM contacts',
  crmOpportunities: 'CRM opportunities',
  crmFollowUps: 'CRM follow-up records',
  crmSuggestions: 'CRM assistant suggestions',
};

/**
 * Registers the CRM business resources and the domain service.
 *
 * The composite resource is the grantable unit the seeded permission sets
 * reference, so it must be defined before the authorization provider validates
 * the stored grants at startup. Registration only makes the operations
 * grantable; the seeded sets decide who holds them.
 */
export class CrmProvider extends ServiceProvider<Application> {
  name = '@nocobase/app/crm';

  register(): void {
    this.app.container.singleton(crmServiceToken, (container) =>
      createCrmService(container.resolve(databaseManagerToken)),
    );
  }

  async boot(): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.app.container.has(authorizationToken)) return;
    const authz = this.app.container.resolve(authorizationToken);

    for (const [name, title] of Object.entries(CRM_COLLECTION_TITLES)) {
      authz.database.collections.add({ name, title });
    }

    const reference = authz.compositeResources.define(crm);
    authz.ui.sections.add({ name: 'crm', title: 'CRM', parent: 'business' });
    authz.ui.place(reference, { section: 'crm' });
  }
}

    E51 · app/server/crm/domain.ts:168–208

    buildSuggestionDrafts 用确定性规则从 customers/followUps 派生建议，注释说明建议需主管批准后才创建跟进。

    /**
 * Derives the assistant's suggestions from what is already in the database.
 *
 * The rules are deterministic on purpose: a supervisor can see exactly why a
 * customer was surfaced, and regenerating produces the same set. A suggestion
 * becomes a follow-up task only after the supervisor approves it.
 */
export function buildSuggestionDrafts(
  input: SuggestionEngineInput,
): SuggestionDraft[] {
  const now = input.now;
  const customerName = new Map(
    input.customers.map((customer) => [customer.id, customer.name]),
  );
  const lastFollowUp = new Map<number, Date>();
  for (const followUp of input.followUps) {
    const at = new Date(followUp.dueAt ?? followUp.createdAt);
    const previous = lastFollowUp.get(followUp.customerId);
    if (!previous || at > previous) {
      lastFollowUp.set(followUp.customerId, at);
    }
  }

  const drafts: SuggestionDraft[] = [];

  for (const followUp of input.followUps) {
    if (followUp.status !== 'pending' || !followUp.dueAt) continue;
    const due = new Date(followUp.dueAt);
    if (due.getTime() >= now.getTime()) continue;
    const name = customerName.get(followUp.customerId) ?? 'this customer';
    const overdueDays = Math.max(1, Math.round(daysBetween(due, now)));
    drafts.push({
      customerId: followUp.customerId,
      opportunityId: null,
      followUpId: followUp.id,
      kind: 'overdue_followup',
      title: `Follow up with ${name}`,
      detail: `The scheduled follow-up is ${overdueDays} day(s) overdue. Confirm the next step with the owner.`,
    });
  }

    原文件 SHA-256：0a4312d1c0ae4b5c91694a0316dd95e06d97aec06afa5098049e21d1f48c4e76

        {

      "symptom": "All 28 tests in tests/logic/app-server.test.ts failed with AppDatabaseTaskError while running database/main seeds: the permission-set seed imported \"../../seed-data/permission-sets.js\" and that seed-data module imported \"../../server/crm/resources.js\".     ```

Field builders are `increments`, `integer`, `bigInt`, `string`, `char`, `text`, `boolean`, `decimal`, `float`, `double`, `date`, `time`, `datetime`, `datetimeTz`, `json`, `blob`, `uuid`, and `native` for a dialect-specific type. Options include `nullable`, `length`, `defaultValue`, `precision` and `scale`; the equivalent chained form (`.notNull()`, `.defaultTo()`, `.unique()`) is also available — pick one style and keep the file consistent.

Relations are `belongsTo`, `hasOne`, `hasMany` and `belongsToMany`. Constraints and indexes are `primary`, `unique`, `foreignKey` and `index`, each taking one field name or an array.

    E138 · artifacts/retro.json:78–84

    retro 记录：crmOpportunities.amount 为 decimal，row decoder 返回字符串（如 '350000'），contract 声明为 number，列表显示 '—'；在 opportunityView 用 numberOrZero 归一。

        {
      "phase": "verify:manual",
      "title": "Opportunity amount rendered as '—' in the list and customer detail",
      "symptom": "Every row of the Opportunities list and the customer-detail opportunity table showed '—' for Amount, even though the API returned values (e.g. '350000') and the edit dialog prefilled the same number correctly.",
      "rootCause": "crmOpportunities.amount is a decimal column. The @nocobase/db row decoder returns decimal fields as strings to preserve precision, so GET /api/crm/opportunities returned amount as '350000'. Every other CRM endpoint spread the raw row (...row), and the client formatAmount(amount: number) guards with Number.isFinite(amount), which is false for a string, so it fell through to '—'. The same leak made the assistant's high-value suggestion text read 'Worth —'.",
      "resolution": "Added an opportunityView(row, owners, customerName) helper in server/crm/service.ts that coerces amount through the existing numberOrZero helper and adds ownerName/customerName, matching the declared contract (OpportunityView.amount: number, schemas.ts z.number()). Used it at all four opportunity-returning sites (customerDetail, listOpportunities, createOpportunity, updateOpportunity) and coerced the rows fed to buildSuggestionDrafts. Added an integration test in tests/logic/app-server.test.ts that signs in to an installed standalone server and asserts every returned amount is a finite number; it fails against the previous code.",
      "status": "resolved"

    客户详情页同时呈现联系人、商机与完整跟进历史；联系人可设主要联系人，商机可关联跟进任务。

    商机阶段推进

    商机支持初步接触、报价、赢单、丢单四阶段，可在详情内联切换，刷新后保持。

    角色数据隔离

    销售仅能查看/修改自己负责的客户与相关商机、跟进，直连他人客户 URL 返回不可见；主管可查看全团队数据。

    导入与助手

    客户名单导入校验输出有效/重复/错误行统计后仅导入有效行；助手建议需主管批准后才创建跟进任务。

    01登录进入 CRM 仪表盘
    →
    02查看各阶段商机数量、预计金额与本周待跟进
    →
    03管理客户、联系人、商机与跟进
    →
    04推进商机阶段至赢单/丢单
    →
    05导入客户名单并处理重复与错误行
    →
    06查看并批准助手建议创建跟进任务
    →
    07以销售身份验证仅见本人客户
    →
    08以主管身份验证查看全团队

    业务顺序示意，操作证据见逐项验收。

    01

    我是销售主管，想给一个10人的销售团队搭建轻量CRM。管理客户、联系人、商机和跟进记录，商机从初步接触、报价推进到赢单或丢单，客户详情能看到相关联系人、商机和完整跟进过程。 销售只能查看和修改自己负责的客户，主管能查看全团队；跟进到期时提醒负责人。支持导入客户名单，指出重复和错误行；首页展示各阶段商机数量、预计成交金额和本周待跟进客户。希望能让助手总结重点客户并建议下一步，建议的跟进任务经我确认后再创建。

    无 console/page 错误或未处理异常

    A1 · 15 条操作 · 12 条观察 · 15 张证据
    Agent 报告通过
    ⌄

    原始验收要求

    我是销售主管，想给一个10人的销售团队搭建轻量CRM。管理客户、联系人、商机和跟进记录，商机从初步接触、报价推进到赢单或丢单，客户详情能看到相关联系人、商机和完整跟进过程。 销售只能查看和修改自己负责的客户，主管能查看全团队；跟进到期时提醒负责人。支持导入客户名单，指出重复和错误行；首页展示各阶段商机数量、预计成交金额和本周待跟进客户。希望能让助手总结重点客户并建议下一步，建议的跟进任务经我确认后再创建。

    实际操作

    1. 以 nocobase 管理员登录后进入 CRM，确认离开登录页并看到业务界面（Dashboard/Customers/Opportunities/Follow-ups）
    2. 在 Customers 编辑 Hanwei Robotics，检查表单原值回填（Name/Level/Industry/Phone/Email/Source/Notes 均正确），将 Notes 改为 'QA edit verification 2026.' 保存后重新打开详情确认已持久化
    3. 新建客户 'QA Test Customer Alpha'（Industry/Phone/Email/Notes），保存后出现在列表且 Owner=Super Admin
    4. 在该客户详情新建联系人 'QA Contact One' 并勾选 Primary contact，保存后联系人表格显示 Primary=Yes
    5. 在同一客户详情新建商机 'QA Opportunity Alpha'（Amount=123456），保存后出现在 Opportunities 列表；在详情内用 Stage 下拉将商机由 Initial contact 改为 Quote 并保存，确认列表 Stage=Quote；再次内联改为 Won 并保存，刷新后 Stage=Won（另有样例初始接触/报价/赢单/丢单各阶段数据）
    6. 在 Follow-ups 页新建跟进 'QA follow-up: confirm next steps.'，关联客户与商机、设置到期时间，保存后列表出现该记录（Owner=Super Admin）；对记录点击 Mark done，列表在 Done 过滤下显示 Status=Done
    7. 在 Hanwei Robotics 详情页确认同时展示 Contacts（2 条）、Opportunities（2 条）与完整 Follow-ups（含 Pending/Overdue 与 Done）历史
    8. Dashboard 显示各阶段商机数量与金额（Initial contact 2/305,000、Quote 2/1,200,000、Won 3/333,456、Lost 2/120,000）、Opportunities/Open pipeline amount/Won amount、Pending/Overdue follow-ups 及 'Follow-ups due this week' 列表，并在新建商机/跟进后数字动态更新
    9. Dashboard 的 Assistant suggestions 点击 Approve（Overdue follow-up - Hanwei Robotics）：Pending follow-ups 由 4 变为 5、Pending suggestions 由 2 变为 1，'Follow-ups due this week' 新增该任务，确认建议任务仅在批准后创建
    10. 在 Customers 页使用 Import：点 Load example 载入含重复与错误行的名单，点 Check rows 显示 '1 valid / 1 duplicate / 1 error(s)'（Duplicate: Already exists: Nimbus Robotics；Error: Email is not valid）；点 'Import 1 valid row(s)' 提示 'Import complete 1 customer(s) created.' 并出现在列表
    11. 通过管理员 User management 新建销售账号 Factory QA（CRM sales representative）并重置 Alex Li（CRM sales representative）、Morgan Chen（CRM sales supervisor）密码用于角色验收
    12. 以销售 Alex Li 登录并完整重新加载，Customers 仅见自己负责的 3 条（Hanwei/Qingyun/Beihai），直接打开他人客户 URL（/customers/4）返回 'The customer does not exist.'；可编辑自己客户；Dashboard 仅统计本人商机/客户并显示本人 Overdue follow-up 提醒
    13. 以销售 Factory QA 登录，Customers 初始为 'No customers yet.'，可新建并编辑自己负责的客户；Opportunities/Follow-ups 仅见本人数据（空）；访问 /settings/users 返回 'No settings available'
    14. 以主管 Morgan Chen 登录并重新加载，Customers 显示全团队 9 条（Owner 含 Factory QA/Super Admin/Alex Li/Bella Wang），Dashboard 汇总全团队商机/客户与本周/逾期跟进
    15. 全程检查 console 与 page errors，无 Something went wrong、空白页、未处理异常或非预期 4xx/5xx

    观察结果

    1. 登录后进入业务界面，CRM 导航含 Dashboard/Customers/Opportunities/Follow-ups
    2. 客户编辑前原值全部正确回填，修改 Notes 保存后重开详情仍显示修改值
    3. 新建客户/联系人/商机/跟进均成功提交并持久化，联系人 Primary=Yes
    4. 商机 Stage 由 Initial contact→Quote→Won 逐级保存并在刷新后保持
    5. 跟进可标记 Done，Done 过滤下可见
    6. 客户详情同时展示 Contacts、Opportunities、Follow-ups（含 Overdue 与 Done）
    7. Dashboard 展示各阶段商机数量与金额、预计成交金额（Open pipeline/Won）、本周待跟进客户
    8. Assistant suggestions 批准后新增跟进任务（Pending 4→5，Suggestions 2→1），未批准前无新增任务
    9. Import 校验输出 1 valid/1 duplicate/1 error 并成功导入有效行
    10. Alex Li 仅见本人 3 客户，他人客户直接 URL 不可见；主管 Morgan Chen 见全团队 9 客户
    11. 销售账号无 Settings 访问权限（No settings available）
    12. 无 console/page 错误或未处理异常

    来源：verify-4/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed
